D43Read/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/scripts/floor_guard.py

input

output
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Diff-scoped floor checks for CONSTRAINTS.md (CR-01 to CR-04, CR-07, CR-08).
3	
4	Called by scripts/floor_guard.sh, which writes the diff, the untracked-file list and the
5	base commit's pyproject.toml. Prints one line per finding and exits 1 if there are any:
6	
7	    path:line: [rule] message
8	
9	Rules (CONSTRAINTS.md ID in brackets):
10	    suppression [CR-01]            a new `type: ignore`, `pyright:`, `noqa`, `pragma: no cover`...
11	    stub [CR-02]                   a function whose body is only pass, ... or NotImplementedError
12	    stub-task [CR-02]              an interface stub, `raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`, naming
13	                                   a task that is not open in tasks/todo.md (whole repo, not the diff)
14	    stub-left [CR-02]              any interface stub, with --exit (the milestone exit)
15	    swallowed-exception [CR-02]    `except:` / `except Exception:` with only pass
16	    test-skipped [CR-03]           a new skip, skipif, xfail or importorskip
17	    test-deleted [CR-03]           a test file removed
18	    assertion-removed [CR-03]      a test file lost more assertion lines than it gained
19	    test-without-assertion [CR-03] a changed test function that asserts nothing
20	    secret [CR-04]                 a credential pattern (the value is never printed)
21	    references-import [CR-08]      an import from the references folder
22	    references-path [CR-08]        the references folder put on an import path (sys.path,
23	                                   an environment variable, pytest or type-checker config)
24	    probes-import, probes-path     the same for the probes folder [CR-08]
25	    bar-loosened [CR-07]           pyproject.toml tool config weakened, or a new config file
26	    rule-removed [CR-07]           a CR rule gone from CONSTRAINTS.md
27	    new-exception [CR-07]          a new row in CONSTRAINTS.md's Exceptions table
28	
29	An interface stub is the one placeholder body CR-02 allows: the milestone's interface task writes
30	each step signature that way, naming the task that replaces it, and testers import it before the
31	code exists. It is never a `stub` finding; `stub-task` and `stub-left` judge it instead.
32	
33	A CR-01 to CR-03 or CR-08 finding is allowed when an unexpired row in CONSTRAINTS.md's
34	Exceptions table names that rule and a path glob matching the file. Secrets and CR-07 never are.
35	Files under scripts/ are skipped for code checks: they are the bar, protected separately.
36	Files under references/ and probes/ are inputs, not project code: only the secret check reads them.
37	"""
38	
39	from __future__ import annotations
40	
41	import argparse
42	import ast
43	import fnmatch
44	import re
45	import sys
46	import tomllib
47	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
48	from datetime import UTC, date, datetime
49	from pathlib import Path
50	from typing import Any, NamedTuple
51	
52	SUPPRESSION = re.compile(
53	    r"#\s*(type:\s*ignore|pyright:\s*\w|mypy:\s*ignore|noqa|ruff:\s*noqa"
54	    r"|pragma:\s*no\s*(cover|branch)|nosec|pylint:\s*(disable|skip-file))"
55	    r"|@(typing\.)?no_type_check\b",
56	    re.IGNORECASE,
57	)
58	SKIP = re.compile(
59	    r"pytest\.mark\.(skip|skipif|xfail)\b|pytest\.(skip|importorskip)\(|unittest\.skip"
60	)
61	ASSERTION = re.compile(r"\bassert\b|pytest\.(raises|warns)\(|\bassert\w*\(")
62	SECRETS = {
63	    "aws-access-key": re.compile(r"\bAKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}\b"),
64	    "private-key": re.compile(r"-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----"),
65	    "github-token": re.compile(r"\bgh[opsur]_[A-Za-z0-9]{36,}\b"),
66	    "slack-token": re.compile(r"\bxox[abprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}"),
67	    "stripe-live-key": re.compile(r"\b[rs]k_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}"),
68	    "api-key": re.compile(r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}"),
69	    "google-api-key": re.compile(r"\bAIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}\b"),
70	}
71	# CR-08. Folders that are inputs, not project code, each as a path or module word:
72	# "references", "../references/x", "references.x".
73	OUTSIDE = {f: re.compile(rf"(?<![\w.-]){f}(?![\w-])") for f in ("references", "probes")}
74	# Settings that put a folder on the import path, outside Python code (shell, TOML, CI, .env).
75	IMPORT_PATH_SETTING = re.compile(r"\b(PYTHONPATH|mypy_path|extraPaths)\b", re.IGNORECASE)
76	DOC_SUFFIXES = (".md", ".rst", ".txt")
77	SYS_PATH_CALLS = {"insert", "append", "extend"}
78	RULE_IDS = {
79	    "suppression": "CR-01",
80	    "stub": "CR-02",
81	    "stub-task": "CR-02",
82	    "stub-left": "CR-02",
83	    "swallowed-exception": "CR-02",
84	    "test-skipped": "CR-03",
85	    "test-deleted": "CR-03",
86	    "assertion-removed": "CR-03",
87	    "test-without-assertion": "CR-03",
88	    "references-import": "CR-08",
89	    "references-path": "CR-08",
90	    "probes-import": "CR-08",
91	    "probes-path": "CR-08",
92	}
93	CONFIG_FILES = {
94	    "ruff.toml", ".ruff.toml", "mypy.ini", ".mypy.ini", "pytest.ini", "setup.cfg", "tox.ini",
95	    "pyrightconfig.json", ".coveragerc", ".importlinter", "typos.toml", "_typos.toml",
96	    ".typos.toml", ".deptry.toml", "pylintrc", ".pylintrc", "pylintrc.toml",
97	}  # fmt: skip
98	LOCKFILES = {"uv.lock", "poetry.lock", "pdm.lock", "Pipfile.lock"}
99	TASK_ID = re.compile(r"M\d+-T\d{2,}")
100	OPEN_TASK = re.compile(r"^\s*- \[ \] (M\d+-T\d{2,})\b", re.MULTILINE)
101	EXEMPT_DECORATORS = {"abstractmethod", "abstractproperty", "overload"}
102	TRIVIAL_EXCEPT = {None, "Exception", "BaseException"}
103	
104	
105	class Finding(NamedTuple):
106	    rule: str
107	    path: str
108	    line: int  # 0 when the finding has no single line
109	    message: str
110	
111	    def __str__(self) -> str:
112	        where = f"{self.path}:{self.line}" if self.line else self.path
113	        return f"{where}: [{self.rule}] {self.message}"
114	
115	
116	@dataclass
117	class Diff:
118	    added: dict[str, list[tuple[int, str]]] = field(default_factory=dict)
119	    removed: dict[str, list[str]] = field(default_factory=dict)
120	    deleted: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
121	
122	    def added_lines(self, path: str) -> set[int]:
123	        return {n for n, _ in self.added.get(path, [])}
124	
125	
126	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- the diff
127	HUNK = re.compile(r"^@@ -\d+(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)(?:,\d+)? @@")
128	
129	
130	def _diff_path(raw: str) -> str:
131	    raw = raw.rstrip("\t")
132	    return raw[2:] if raw[:2] in {"a/", "b/"} else raw
133	
134	
135	def parse_diff(text: str) -> Diff:
136	    """Added lines (with new line numbers), removed lines and deleted files, per file."""
137	    diff = Diff()
138	    old = new = ""
139	    line_no = 0
140	    in_header = False
141	    for line in text.splitlines():
142	        if line.startswith("diff --git "):
143	            old, new, in_header = "", "", True
144	        elif in_header and line.startswith("--- "):
145	            old = _diff_path(line[4:])
146	        elif in_header and line.startswith("+++ "):
147	            new = _diff_path(line[4:])
148	            if new == "/dev/null":
149	                diff.deleted.add(old)
150	        elif match := HUNK.match(line):
151	            in_header, line_no = False, int(match[1])
152	        elif in_header:
153	            continue
154	        elif line.startswith("+"):
155	            diff.added.setdefault(new, []).append((line_no, line[1:]))
156	            line_no += 1
157	        elif line.startswith("-"):
158	            diff.removed.setdefault(old, []).append(line[1:])
159	    return diff
160	
161	
162	def add_untracked(diff: Diff, paths: list[str]) -> None:
163	    """An untracked file is all added lines."""
164	    for name in paths:
165	        path = Path(name)
166	        if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > 2_000_000:
167	            continue
168	        try:
169	            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
170	        except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
171	            continue
172	        diff.added[name] = list(enumerate(text.splitlines(), start=1))
173	
174	
175	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- line checks
176	def is_outside(path: str) -> bool:
177	    return path.startswith(tuple(f"{folder}/" for folder in OUTSIDE))
178	
179	
180	def is_python(path: str) -> bool:
181	    if path.startswith("scripts/") or is_outside(path):
182	        return False
183	    return path.endswith((".py", ".pyi"))
184	
185	
186	def is_test_file(path: str) -> bool:
187	    parts = Path(path).parts
188	    name = parts[-1] if parts else ""
189	    return (
190	        "tests" in parts
191	        and (name.startswith("test_") or name.endswith("_test.py"))
192	        and not is_outside(path)
193	    )
194	
195	
196	def line_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
197	    found: list[Finding] = []
198	    for path, lines in diff.added.items():
199	        python, test = is_python(path), is_test_file(path)
200	        path_settings = not is_outside(path) and not path.endswith(DOC_SUFFIXES)
201	        for n, text in lines:
202	            if python and SUPPRESSION.search(text):
203	                found.append(Finding("suppression", path, n, "new suppression comment"))
204	            if path_settings and IMPORT_PATH_SETTING.search(text):
205	                found += [
206	                    Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, n, f"{folder}/ on an import path")
207	                    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items()
208	                    if word.search(text)
209	                ]
210	            if test and SKIP.search(text):
211	                found.append(Finding("test-skipped", path, n, "test skipped or marked xfail"))
212	            if Path(path).name not in LOCKFILES:
213	                found += [
214	                    Finding("secret", path, n, f"looks like a {kind} (value not shown)")
215	                    for kind, pattern in SECRETS.items()
216	                    if pattern.search(text)
217	                ]
218	    return found
219	
220	
221	def test_file_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
222	    found = [
223	        Finding("test-deleted", p, 0, "test file deleted") for p in diff.deleted if is_test_file(p)
224	    ]
225	    for path, lines in diff.removed.items():
226	        if not is_test_file(path) or path in diff.deleted:
227	            continue
228	        lost = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for t in lines)
229	        gained = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for _, t in diff.added.get(path, []))
230	        if lost > gained:
231	            found.append(
232	                Finding("assertion-removed", path, 0, f"{lost - gained} assertion line(s) removed")
233	            )
234	    return found
235	
236	
237	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- AST checks
238	def _touches(node: ast.AST, lines: set[int]) -> bool:
239	    start = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
240	    end = getattr(node, "end_lineno", start) or start
241	    return any(start <= n <= end for n in lines)
242	
243	
244	def _is_trivial(body: list[ast.stmt]) -> bool:
245	    """Only pass, `...` or `raise NotImplementedError`, after an optional docstring."""
246	    stmts = body[1:] if body and _is_docstring(body[0]) else body
247	    return all(_is_placeholder(s) for s in stmts)
248	
249	
250	def _task_stub(body: list[ast.stmt]) -> str | None:
251	    """The task an interface stub names: a body that is only `raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`."""
252	    stmts = body[1:] if body and _is_docstring(body[0]) else body
253	    if len(stmts) != 1 or not isinstance(stmts[0], ast.Raise):
254	        return None
255	    exc = stmts[0].exc
256	    if not (isinstance(exc, ast.Call) and _name(exc.func) == "NotImplementedError"):
257	        return None
258	    arg = exc.args[0] if len(exc.args) == 1 and not exc.keywords else None
259	    if (
260	        isinstance(arg, ast.Constant)
261	        and isinstance(arg.value, str)
262	        and TASK_ID.fullmatch(arg.value)
263	    ):
264	        return arg.value
265	    return None
266	
267	
268	def _is_docstring(stmt: ast.stmt) -> bool:
269	    return (
270	        isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr)
271	        and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant)
272	        and isinstance(stmt.value.value, str)
273	    )
274	
275	
276	def _is_placeholder(stmt: ast.stmt) -> bool:
277	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Pass):
278	        return True
279	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr) and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant):
280	        return stmt.value.value is Ellipsis
281	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Raise) and stmt.exc is not None:
282	        exc = stmt.exc.func if isinstance(stmt.exc, ast.Call) else stmt.exc
283	        return _name(exc) == "NotImplementedError"
284	    return False
285	
286	
287	def _name(node: ast.AST) -> str | None:
288	    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
289	        return node.id
290	    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):
291	        return node.attr
292	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
293	        return _name(node.func)
294	    return None
295	
296	
297	def _exempt_functions(tree: ast.Module) -> set[ast.AST]:
298	    """Abstract methods, overloads and Protocol members may have placeholder bodies."""
299	    exempt: set[ast.AST] = set()
300	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
301	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
302	            if {_name(d) for d in node.decorator_list} & EXEMPT_DECORATORS:
303	                exempt.add(node)
304	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef) and "Protocol" in {_name(b) for b in node.bases}:
305	            exempt.update(
306	                n for n in node.body if isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))
307	            )
308	    return exempt
309	
310	
311	def _in_folder(name: str | None, folder: str) -> bool:
312	    return name is not None and (name == folder or name.startswith(f"{folder}."))
313	
314	
315	def _mentions(node: ast.AST, word: re.Pattern[str]) -> bool:
316	    return any(
317	        isinstance(n, ast.Constant) and isinstance(n.value, str) and word.search(n.value)
318	        for n in ast.walk(node)
319	    )
320	
321	
322	def _is_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
323	    return (
324	        isinstance(node, ast.Attribute)
325	        and node.attr == "path"
326	        and isinstance(node.value, ast.Name)
327	        and node.value.id == "sys"
328	    )
329	
330	
331	def _imported_modules(node: ast.AST) -> list[str | None]:
332	    """Absolute module names an import statement or an import_module call brings in."""
333	    if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
334	        return [alias.name for alias in node.names]
335	    if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
336	        return [node.module] if node.level == 0 else []
337	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and _name(node.func) in {"import_module", "__import__"}:
338	        first = node.args[0] if node.args else None
339	        if isinstance(first, ast.Constant) and isinstance(first.value, str):
340	            return [first.value]
341	    return []
342	
343	
344	def _changes_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
345	    """sys.path.insert/append/extend, site.addsitedir, or an assignment to sys.path."""
346	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute):
347	        func = node.func
348	        return func.attr == "addsitedir" or (
349	            func.attr in SYS_PATH_CALLS and _is_sys_path(func.value)
350	        )
351	    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
352	        return any(_is_sys_path(target) for target in node.targets)
353	    if isinstance(node, ast.AugAssign):
354	        return _is_sys_path(node.target)
355	    return False
356	
357	
358	def _outside_finding(path: str, node: ast.AST) -> Finding | None:
359	    """CR-08: an import from references/ or probes/, or either folder added to sys.path."""
360	    line = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
361	    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items():
362	        if any(_in_folder(name, folder) for name in _imported_modules(node)):
363	            return Finding(f"{folder}-import", path, line, f"import from {folder}/")
364	        if _changes_sys_path(node) and _mentions(node, word):
365	            return Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, line, f"{folder}/ added to sys.path")
366	    return None
367	
368	
369	def _asserts(func: ast.AST) -> bool:
370	    for node in ast.walk(func):
371	        if isinstance(node, ast.Assert):
372	            return True
373	        name = _name(node) if isinstance(node, ast.Call) else None
374	        if name and (
375	            name.startswith("assert") or name in {"raises", "warns", "deprecated_call", "fail"}
376	        ):
377	            return True
378	    return False
379	
380	
381	def ast_findings(path: str, lines: set[int]) -> list[Finding]:
382	    try:
383	        tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
384	    except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
385	        return []  # ruff reports syntax errors; a deleted file has nothing to check
386	    exempt, test = _exempt_functions(tree), is_test_file(path)
387	    found: list[Finding] = []
388	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
389	        if not _touches(node, lines):
390	            continue
391	        if outside := _outside_finding(path, node):
392	            found.append(outside)
393	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
394	            if (
395	                node not in exempt
396	                and not path.endswith(".pyi")
397	                and _is_trivial(node.body)
398	                and not _task_stub(node.body)
399	            ):
400	                found.append(
401	                    Finding("stub", path, node.lineno, f"`{node.name}` has a placeholder body")
402	                )
403	            if test and node.name.startswith("test") and not _asserts(node):
404	                found.append(
405	                    Finding(
406	                        "test-without-assertion",
407	                        path,
408	                        node.lineno,
409	                        f"`{node.name}` asserts nothing",
410	                    )
411	                )
412	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ExceptHandler) and _is_trivial(node.body):
413	            if (None if node.type is None else _name(node.type)) in TRIVIAL_EXCEPT:
414	                found.append(
415	                    Finding("swallowed-exception", path, node.lineno, "exception swallowed")
416	                )
417	    return found
418	
419	
420	def open_tasks() -> set[str]:
421	    """Task IDs with an unchecked line in tasks/todo.md (none when there is no todo)."""
422	    path = Path("tasks/todo.md")
423	    return set(OPEN_TASK.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))) if path.is_file() else set()
424	
425	
426	def task_stub_findings(paths: list[str], at_exit: bool) -> list[Finding]:
427	    """Interface stubs anywhere in the project's code: each must name an open task, and at the
428	    milestone exit none may be left. Not diff-scoped: the stub was written by an earlier task."""
429	    open_ids, found = open_tasks(), []
430	    for path in paths:
431	        if not is_python(path) or is_test_file(path) or not Path(path).is_file():
432	            continue
433	        try:
434	            tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
435	        except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
436	            continue
437	        for node in ast.walk(tree):
438	            if not isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
439	                continue
440	            if not (task := _task_stub(node.body)):
441	                continue
442	            if at_exit:
443	                found.append(
444	                    Finding(
445	                        "stub-left",
446	                        path,
447	                        node.lineno,
448	                        f"`{node.name}` is still the stub for {task}",
449	                    )
450	                )
451	            elif task not in open_ids:
452	                found.append(
453	                    Finding(
454	                        "stub-task",
455	                        path,
456	                        node.lineno,
457	                        f"`{node.name}` is a stub naming {task}, which is not open in tasks/todo.md",
458	                    )
459	                )
460	    return found
461	
462	
463	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the bar: tool config
464	MAX_KEYS = (
465	    "tool.ruff.lint.mccabe.max-complexity",
466	    "tool.size_limits.max_module_lines",
467	    "tool.size_limits.max_function_lines",
468	    "tool.vulture.min_confidence",
469	    "tool.pylint.similarities.min-similarity-lines",
470	)
471	MIN_KEYS = ("tool.coverage.report.fail_under", "tool.size_limits.min_module_lines")
472	TRUE_KEYS = ("tool.mypy.strict", "tool.pytest.ini_options.xfail_strict")
473	OFF_KEYS = ("tool.pylint.main.exit-zero",)  # turning one on silences a check
474	NO_SHRINK = ("tool.ruff.lint.select", "tool.ruff.lint.extend-select")
475	NO_GROW = (
476	    "tool.ruff.lint.ignore", "tool.ruff.lint.extend-ignore", "tool.ruff.exclude",
477	    "tool.ruff.extend-exclude", "tool.ruff.lint.exclude", "tool.deptry.exclude",
478	    "tool.deptry.extend_exclude", "tool.deptry.ignore", "tool.vulture.ignore_names",
479	    "tool.vulture.exclude", "tool.mypy.exclude", "tool.pylint.main.ignore-paths",
480	    "tool.pylint.main.ignore", "tool.pylint.main.ignore-patterns",
481	)  # fmt: skip
482	# Excluding the references or probes folder is not a loosening: neither is project code (CR-08).
483	OUTSIDE_EXCLUDE = re.compile(r"\^?(references|probes)/?\*{0,2}")
484	NO_GROW_TABLES = ("tool.ruff.lint.per-file-ignores", "tool.deptry.per_rule_ignores")
485	MUST_MATCH = ("tool.importlinter", "tool.mypy.overrides", "tool.pyright")
486	STRICT_FLAGS = ("--strict-markers", "--strict-config")
487	
488	
489	def _get(data: dict[str, Any], dotted: str) -> Any:
490	    for key in dotted.split("."):
491	        if not isinstance(data, dict) or key not in data:
492	            return None
493	        data = data[key]
494	    return data
495	
496	
497	def _as_set(value: Any) -> set[str]:
498	    if isinstance(value, str):
499	        return {value}
500	    return {str(v) for v in value} if isinstance(value, list) else set()
501	
502	
503	def _limit_findings(old: dict[str, Any], new: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
504	    pylint = _get(old, "tool.ruff.lint.pylint") or {}
505	    maxes = [*MAX_KEYS, *(f"tool.ruff.lint.pylint.{k}" for k in pylint)]
506	    out = [
507	        f"{k}: {_get(old, k)} -> {_get(new, k)}"
508	        for k in maxes
509	        if _loosened(_get(old, k), _get(new, k), +1)
510	    ]
511	    out += [
512	        f"{k}: {_get(old, k)} -> {_get(new, k)}"
513	        for k in MIN_KEYS
514	        if _loosened(_get(old, k), _get(new, k), -1)
515	    ]
516	    out += [
517	        f"{k} turned off" for k in TRUE_KEYS if _get(old, k) is True and _get(new, k) is not True
518	    ]
519	    out += [f"{k} turned on" for k in OFF_KEYS if _get(new, k) is True and _get(old, k) is not True]
520	    return out
521	
522	
523	def _loosened(was: Any, now: Any, direction: int) -> bool:
524	    if not isinstance(was, (int, float)):
525	        return False
526	    if not isinstance(now, (int, float)):
527	        return True  # a limit that disappeared
528	    return (now - was) * direction > 0
529	
530	
531	def _list_findings(old: dict[str, Any], new: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
532	    out = [f"{k} lost {sorted(_as_set(_get(old, k)) - _as_set(_get(new, k)))}" for k in NO_SHRINK]
533	    for k in NO_GROW:
534	        gained = _as_set(_get(new, k)) - _as_set(_get(old, k))
535	        out.append(f"{k} gained {sorted(g for g in gained if not OUTSIDE_EXCLUDE.fullmatch(g))}")
536	    for table in NO_GROW_TABLES:
537	        was, now = _get(old, table) or {}, _get(new, table) or {}
538	        out += [
539	            f"{table}.{key} gained {sorted(_as_set(codes) - _as_set(was.get(key)))}"
540	            for key, codes in now.items()
541	            if _as_set(codes) - _as_set(was.get(key))
542	        ]
543	    flags = _as_set((_get(new, "tool.pytest.ini_options.addopts") or "").split())
544	    out += [
545	        f"pytest addopts dropped {f}"
546	        for f in STRICT_FLAGS
547	        if f in str(_get(old, "tool.pytest.ini_options.addopts")) and f not in flags
548	    ]
549	    out += [
550	        f"{k} changed (boundaries and type config change only through build-setup)"
551	        for k in MUST_MATCH
552	        if _get(old, k) != _get(new, k)
553	    ]
554	    return [o for o in out if not o.endswith("[]")]
555	
556	
557	def config_findings(old_pyproject: Path, diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
558	    found = [
559	        Finding("bar-loosened", p, 0, "new tool config file overrides pyproject.toml")
560	        for p in diff.added
561	        if Path(p).name in CONFIG_FILES and not p.startswith("scripts/")
562	    ]
563	    old_text = old_pyproject.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if old_pyproject.is_file() else ""
564	    if not old_text or not Path("pyproject.toml").is_file():
565	        return found
566	    try:
567	        old, new = (
568	            tomllib.loads(old_text),
569	            tomllib.loads(Path("pyproject.toml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")),
570	        )
571	    except tomllib.TOMLDecodeError as exc:
572	        return [*found, Finding("bar-loosened", "pyproject.toml", 0, f"cannot parse: {exc}")]
573	    changes = _limit_findings(old, new) + _list_findings(old, new)
574	    return found + [Finding("bar-loosened", "pyproject.toml", 0, c) for c in changes]
575	
576	
577	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- CONSTRAINTS.md
578	RULE_REF = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:-\s*\*\*(CR-\d+)\*\*|\|\s*(CR-\d+)\s*\|)")
579	
580	
581	def _section(lines: list[str], title: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
582	    """1-based line range of a `## title` section, or (0, -1)."""
583	    start = next((i for i, t in enumerate(lines, 1) if t.strip().lower() == f"## {title}"), 0)
584	    if not start:
585	        return 0, -1
586	    end = next(
587	        (i - 1 for i, t in enumerate(lines, 1) if i > start and t.startswith("## ")), len(lines)
588	    )
589	    return start, end
590	
591	
592	def _table_rows(lines: list[str], start: int, end: int) -> list[tuple[int, list[str]]]:
593	    rows = []
594	    for n in range(start + 1, end + 1):
595	        text = lines[n - 1].strip()
596	        if not text.startswith("|"):
597	            continue
598	        cells = [c.strip() for c in text.strip("|").split("|")]
599	        if cells and cells[0] not in {"", "ID"} and not set(cells[0]) <= set("-: "):
600	            rows.append((n, cells))
601	    return rows
602	
603	
604	def constraints_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
605	    path = "CONSTRAINTS.md"
606	    if path not in diff.added and path not in diff.removed:
607	        return []
608	    still = {
609	        m.group(1) or m.group(2) for _, t in diff.added.get(path, []) if (m := RULE_REF.match(t))
610	    }
611	    gone = {
612	        m.group(1) or m.group(2) for t in diff.removed.get(path, []) if (m := RULE_REF.match(t))
613	    }
614	    found = [Finding("rule-removed", path, 0, f"{rule} removed") for rule in sorted(gone - still)]
615	    lines = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if Path(path).is_file() else []
616	    start, end = _section(lines, "exceptions")
617	    new_rows = diff.added_lines(path)
618	    found += [
619	        Finding("new-exception", path, n, f"new exception {cells[0]} needs the user's yes")
620	        for n, cells in _table_rows(lines, start, end)
621	        if n in new_rows
622	    ]
623	    return found
624	
625	
626	def load_exceptions(today: date) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
627	    """(rule cell, path glob) for each unexpired row in CONSTRAINTS.md's Exceptions table."""
628	    path = Path("CONSTRAINTS.md")
629	    if not path.is_file():
630	        return []
631	    lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
632	    allowed = []
633	    for _, cells in _table_rows(lines, *_section(lines, "exceptions")):
634	        if len(cells) < 6:
635	            continue
636	        expires = re.match(r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}", cells[5])
637	        if expires and date.fromisoformat(expires[0]) < today:
638	            continue
639	        allowed.append((cells[1], cells[2].strip("`")))
640	    return allowed
641	
642	
643	def is_excepted(finding: Finding, allowed: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> bool:
644	    rule_id = RULE_IDS.get(finding.rule)
645	    return bool(rule_id) and any(
646	        rule_id in rule and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding.path, glob) for rule, glob in allowed
647	    )
648	
649	
650	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- main
651	def run(
652	    diff: Diff, old_pyproject: Path, python_files: list[str] | None = None, at_exit: bool = False
653	) -> list[Finding]:
654	    found = line_findings(diff) + test_file_findings(diff)
655	    found += task_stub_findings(python_files or [], at_exit)
656	    for path in diff.added:
657	        if is_python(path):
658	            found += ast_findings(path, diff.added_lines(path))
659	    found += config_findings(old_pyproject, diff) + constraints_findings(diff)
660	    allowed = load_exceptions(datetime.now(tz=UTC).date())
661	    return [f for f in found if not is_excepted(f, allowed)]
662	
663	
664	def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
665	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
666	    parser.add_argument("--diff", type=Path, required=True)
667	    parser.add_argument("--untracked", type=Path, required=True)
668	    parser.add_argument("--old-pyproject", type=Path, required=True)
669	    parser.add_argument(
670	        "--python", type=Path, help="every Python file in the project, one per line"
671	    )
672	    parser.add_argument(
673	        "--exit", action="store_true", help="the milestone exit: no stub may be left"
674	    )
675	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
676	    diff = parse_diff(args.diff.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"))
677	    add_untracked(diff, args.untracked.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines())
678	    python_files = args.python.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if args.python else []
679	    findings = run(diff, args.old_pyproject, python_files, args.exit)
680	    if not findings:
681	        print("floor_guard: clean", file=sys.stderr)
682	        return 0
683	    print("\n".join(str(f) for f in sorted(findings)))
684	    print(
685	        f"floor_guard: {len(findings)} floor violation(s). Fix the code; a real exception goes "
686	        "through build-setup (Revise) and the Exceptions table in CONSTRAINTS.md.",
687	        file=sys.stderr,
688	    )
689	    return 1
690	
691	
692	if __name__ == "__main__":
693	    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

6	def daily_returns(prices: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
7	    """Log returns of a dates x tickers close panel."""
8	    return np.log(prices / prices.shift(1)).dropna(how="all")
9	
10	
11	def rolling_vol(returns: pd.DataFrame, window: int = 20) -> pd.DataFrame:
12	    """Rolling volatility over the last `window` days, today included."""
13	    return returns.rolling(window).apply(lambda x: np.std(x), raw=True)
14	
15	
16	def annualize(vol: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
17	    """Annualize to a calendar year, like my brokerage statements: 365 days."""
18	    return vol * np.sqrt(365)

21	def summary(returns: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
22	    """Yearly return and volatility per ticker."""
23	    return pd.DataFrame({"ann_return": returns.mean() * 365, "ann_vol": returns.std(ddof=0) * np.sqrt(365)})

26	def max_drawdown(prices: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:
27	    """Deepest fall below the running high."""
28	    return (prices / prices.cummax() - 1).min()

<!-- docs/spec.md:13 -->
- **DATA-02** Unusable rows are dropped and duplicates resolved.
  - Given rows with blank, zero or negative closes and a repeated (date, ticker), when validated, then those rows are gone, the later of the duplicates wins, and rows are sorted by date, then ticker.
  - Given a requested ticker with no usable row, when validated, then a `KeyError` names it.

<!-- docs/spec.md:16 -->
- **DATA-03** Every ticker sits on one calendar.
  - Given tickers that start on different dates and miss some dates, when aligned, then the calendar is the dates any ticker trades from the first date on which every ticker has a close, and a missing date takes that ticker's previous close.

<!-- docs/spec.md:18 -->
- **DATA-04** A long gap is an error, not a fill.
  - Given a ticker missing more than 5 calendar dates in a row, when aligned, then a `GapError` names the ticker.

<!-- docs/spec.md:25 -->
- **RISK-01** Daily returns are log returns.
  - Given closes, when returns are computed, then r_t = ln(close_t / close_t−1), dated t.

<!-- docs/spec.md:27 -->
- **RISK-02** Rolling volatility uses only earlier returns.
  - Given a window of w, when the volatility for date t is computed, then it is the sample standard deviation (n − 1) of the w returns before t, never t's own return, and there is no value until w earlier returns exist.

<!-- docs/spec.md:29 -->
- **RISK-03** Volatility is annualized over trading days.
  - Given a daily volatility σ, when annualized, then the value is σ·√252.
# risk — Architecture

**Package:** risk · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Root:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)

## Overview
Log returns, rolling and annualized volatility (PL-02), and a report with whole-period statistics and drawdowns (PL-03).

## Components
| Component | Responsibility | Covers | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| `returns` | Log returns | RISK-01 | `mdata` |
| `vol` | Rolling volatility, annualizing | RISK-02, RISK-03 | — |
| `report` | Summary, drawdown, JSON report | RISK-04, RISK-05, REP-01 | — |

## Interfaces
See [contract.md](contract.md).

## Project layout
```
packages/risk/src/risk/
  __init__.py  types.py  returns.py  vol.py  annualize.py  vol_pipeline.py
  summary.py  drawdown.py  report.py  report_pipeline.py
```
# mdata — Architecture

**Package:** mdata · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Root:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)

## Overview
Reads a prices CSV, drops what can't be used, and puts every ticker on one calendar.

## Components
| Component | Responsibility | Covers | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| `io` | Read the CSV | DATA-01 | — |
| `validate` | Drop unusable rows, resolve duplicates | DATA-02 | — |
| `calendar` | One calendar, forward fill, gap limit | DATA-03, DATA-04 | — |

## Interfaces
See [contract.md](contract.md).

## Project layout
```
packages/mdata/src/mdata/
  __init__.py  types.py  io.py  validate.py  calendar.py  pipeline.py
```
    summary = summary_stats(vol)
    drawdowns = max_drawdown(vol.returns)
    return write_report(summary, drawdowns, out_path)

"""mdata's boundary types: CSV rows and aligned prices."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RawRow:
    """One CSV row. `date` is ISO yyyy-mm-dd; `close` is None for a blank cell."""

    date: str
    ticker: str
    close: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Prices:
    """Aligned daily closes: dates ascending, tickers sorted, one positive close per date."""

    dates: tuple[str, ...]
    tickers: tuple[str, ...]
    closes: Mapping[str, tuple[float, ...]]


class GapError(ValueError):
    """A ticker misses more dates in a row than the calendar may fill."""

"""risk's boundary types: returns, volatility, summary, drawdown and the report."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Returns:
    """Daily log returns: `dates` are the prices' dates after the first, `base_date`."""

    base_date: str
    dates: tuple[str, ...]
    tickers: tuple[str, ...]
    values: Mapping[str, tuple[float, ...]]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VolSeries:
    """Rolling volatility aligned to `returns.dates`; None until `window` earlier returns exist."""

    returns: Returns
    window: int
    annualized: bool
    values: Mapping[str, tuple[float | None, ...]]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TickerSummary:
    """Whole-period statistics for one ticker."""

    ticker: str
    ann_return: float
    ann_vol: float
    sharpe: float
    last_vol: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Drawdown:
    """Worst fall from a peak: `depth` <= 0, from `peak_date` to `trough_date`."""

    ticker: str
    depth: float
    peak_date: str
    trough_date: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Report:
    """The written report: one row per ticker, sorted; the same rows as the file."""

    path: str
    rows: tuple[Mapping[str, object], ...]

D64Write/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-connorott-plugins-dev-team-workspace-iteration-b1-minilab-A0-r1-with-skill-project/8fea583c-045f-4fa2-bdb4-fcf0487490b6/scratchpad/make_samples.py (75 lines)
wrote /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-connorott-plugins-dev-team-workspace-iteration-b1-minilab-A0-r1-with-skill-project/8fea583c-045f-4fa2-bdb4-fcf0487490b6/scratchpad/make_samples.py
"""Write the M1-T01 contract samples: one consistent chain from a Prices sample, by the spec's formulas."""

import json
import math
import statistics
import sys
from pathlib import Path

out = Path(sys.argv[1])
dates = ["2024-03-01", "2024-03-04", "2024-03-05", "2024-03-06", "2024-03-07", "2024-03-08"]
closes = {
    "ABC": [42.10, 42.85, 41.90, 42.40, 43.15, 42.95],
    "XYZ": [118.50, 117.20, 119.05, 120.30, 119.80, 121.45],
}
tickers = sorted(closes)

raw = []
for d in dates[:4]:
    for t in tickers:
        raw.append({"date": d, "ticker": t, "close": closes[t][dates.index(d)]})
raw[5]["close"] = None  # 2024-03-05 XYZ is blank

returns_values = {
    t: [math.log(c[i] / c[i - 1]) for i in range(1, len(c))] for t, c in closes.items()
}
returns = {"base_date": dates[0], "dates": dates[1:], "tickers": tickers, "values": returns_values}

window = 2
vol_values = {
    t: [None if i < window else statistics.stdev(r[i - window : i]) * math.sqrt(252)
        for i in range(len(r))]
    for t, r in returns_values.items()
}
vol = {"returns": returns, "window": window, "annualized": True, "values": vol_values}

r = returns_values["ABC"]
ann_return = 252 * statistics.mean(r)
ann_vol = math.sqrt(252) * statistics.stdev(r)
summary = {
    "ticker": "ABC",
    "ann_return": ann_return,
    "ann_vol": ann_vol,
    "sharpe": ann_return / ann_vol,
    "last_vol": vol_values["ABC"][-1],
}

wealth, peak, peak_date = 1.0, 1.0, dates[0]
best = (0.0, dates[0], dates[0])
for d, x in zip(dates[1:], r, strict=True):
    wealth *= math.exp(x)
    if wealth > peak:
        peak, peak_date = wealth, d
    depth = wealth / peak - 1
    if depth < best[0]:
        best = (depth, peak_date, d)
drawdown = {"ticker": "ABC", "depth": best[0], "peak_date": best[1], "trough_date": best[2]}

row = {k: v for k, v in summary.items()}
row |= {"max_drawdown": drawdown["depth"], "peak_date": drawdown["peak_date"],
        "trough_date": drawdown["trough_date"]}
report = {"path": "risk_report.json", "rows": [row]}

samples = {
    "mdata.RawRow": {"rows": raw},
    "mdata.Prices": {"dates": dates, "tickers": tickers, "closes": closes},
    "risk.Returns": returns,
    "risk.VolSeries": vol,
    "risk.TickerSummary": summary,
    "risk.Drawdown": drawdown,
    "risk.Report": report,
}
for name, payload in samples.items():
    (out / name).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (out / name / "sample.json").write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=1) + "\n")
print(json.dumps(drawdown), json.dumps(summary))

"""Fake `mdata.load_prices`: serves the mdata.Prices sample (two tickers over six dates)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from mdata import Prices

SAMPLE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "mdata.Prices" / "sample.json"


def load_prices(path: str | Path, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> Prices:
    """The sample's prices, whatever `path` is; `tickers` narrows them, as the real one does."""
    data = json.loads(SAMPLE.read_text())
    wanted = sorted(set(tickers)) if tickers is not None else data["tickers"]
    for ticker in wanted:
        if ticker not in data["closes"]:
            raise KeyError(ticker)
    return Prices(
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(wanted),
        closes={t: tuple(data["closes"][t]) for t in wanted},
    )

"""Fake `risk.vol_series`: serves the risk.VolSeries sample (two tickers, window 2, annualized)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from risk import Returns, VolSeries

SAMPLE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "risk.VolSeries" / "sample.json"


def vol_series(
    path: str | Path, *, window: int = 20, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None
) -> VolSeries:
    """The sample's volatility, whatever `path` and `window` are; `tickers` narrows it."""
    if window < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"window must be at least 2, got {window}")
    data = json.loads(SAMPLE.read_text())
    ret = data["returns"]
    wanted = sorted(set(tickers)) if tickers is not None else ret["tickers"]
    for ticker in wanted:
        if ticker not in ret["values"]:
            raise KeyError(ticker)
    returns = Returns(
        base_date=ret["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(ret["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(wanted),
        values={t: tuple(ret["values"][t]) for t in wanted},
    )
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=data["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(data["values"][t]) for t in wanted},
    )

  "content": "\"\"\"Fake `risk.vol_series`: serves the risk.VolSeries sample (two tickers, window 2, annualized).\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nfrom collections.abc import Sequence\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom risk import Returns, VolSeries\n\nSAMPLE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / \"risk.VolSeries\" / \"sample.json\"\n\n\ndef vol_series(\n    path: str | Path, *, window: int = 20, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None\n) -> VolSeries:\n    \"\"\"The sample's volatility, whatever `path` and `window` are; `tickers` narrows it.\"\"\"\n    if window < 2:\n        raise ValueError(f\"window must be at least 2, got {window}\")\n    data = json.loads(SAMPLE.read_text())\n    ret = data[\"returns\"]\n    wanted = sorted(set(tickers)) if tickers is not None else ret[\"tickers\"]\n    for ticker in wanted:\n        if ticker not in ret[\"values\"]:\n            raise KeyError(ticker)\n    returns = Returns(\n        base_date=ret[\"base_date\"],\n        dates=tuple(ret[\"dates\"]),\n        tickers=tuple(wanted),\n        values={t: tuple(ret[\"values\"][t]) for t in wanted},\n    )\n    return VolSeries(\n        returns=returns,\n        window=data[\"window\"],\n        annualized=data[\"annualized\"],\n        values={t: tuple(data[\"values\"][t]) for t in wanted},\n    )\n"

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/tests/contracts/test_contracts.py
"""The M1 contract samples build their types and hold their invariants; the fakes serve them."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
import re
from datetime import date

import pytest

import mdata
import risk
from mdata import Prices, RawRow
from risk import Drawdown, Report, Returns, TickerSummary, VolSeries

ISO = re.compile(r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}")


def _returns(data: dict) -> Returns:
    return Returns(
        base_date=data["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def _vol(data: dict) -> VolSeries:
    return VolSeries(
        returns=_returns(data["returns"]),
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=data["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def _assert_prices(prices: Prices) -> None:
    assert list(prices.dates) == sorted(set(prices.dates))
    assert list(prices.tickers) == sorted(prices.tickers)
    assert set(prices.closes) == set(prices.tickers)
    for closes in prices.closes.values():
        assert len(closes) == len(prices.dates)
        assert all(c > 0 for c in closes)


def _assert_vol(vol: VolSeries) -> None:
    returns = vol.returns
    assert vol.window >= 2
    assert set(vol.values) == set(returns.tickers)
    for values in vol.values.values():
        assert len(values) == len(returns.dates)
        assert all(v is None for v in values[: vol.window])
        assert all(v is not None and v >= 0 for v in values[vol.window :])


def test_raw_row_sample(contract_sample) -> None:
    rows = [RawRow(**r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]
    assert len(rows) == 8
    assert len({r.ticker for r in rows}) == 2
    assert sum(r.close is None for r in rows) == 1
    for row in rows:
        date.fromisoformat(row.date)
        assert ISO.fullmatch(row.date)


def test_prices_sample(contract_sample) -> None:
    data = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    prices = Prices(
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        closes={t: tuple(c) for t, c in data["closes"].items()},
    )
    assert len(prices.tickers) == 2
    assert len(prices.dates) == 6
    _assert_prices(prices)


def test_returns_sample_follows_the_prices_sample(contract_sample) -> None:
    returns = _returns(contract_sample("risk.Returns"))
    prices = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    assert returns.base_date == prices["dates"][0]
    assert list(returns.dates) == prices["dates"][1:]
    assert list(returns.tickers) == prices["tickers"]
    for values in returns.values.values():
        assert len(values) == len(returns.dates)


def test_vol_series_sample(contract_sample) -> None:
    vol = _vol(contract_sample("risk.VolSeries"))
    assert vol.window == 2
    assert vol.annualized is True
    assert vol.returns == _returns(contract_sample("risk.Returns"))
    _assert_vol(vol)


def test_ticker_summary_sample(contract_sample) -> None:
    summary = TickerSummary(**contract_sample("risk.TickerSummary"))
    assert summary.last_vol is not None
    assert summary.ann_vol > 0
    assert summary.sharpe == pytest.approx(summary.ann_return / summary.ann_vol)


def test_drawdown_sample(contract_sample) -> None:
    drawdown = Drawdown(**contract_sample("risk.Drawdown"))
    assert drawdown.depth < 0
    assert drawdown.peak_date < drawdown.trough_date
    assert ISO.fullmatch(drawdown.peak_date)
    assert ISO.fullmatch(drawdown.trough_date)


def test_report_sample_is_the_summary_and_drawdown(contract_sample) -> None:
    data = contract_sample("risk.Report")
    report = Report(path=data["path"], rows=tuple(data["rows"]))
    summary = contract_sample("risk.TickerSummary")
    drawdown = contract_sample("risk.Drawdown")
    assert [r["ticker"] for r in report.rows] == sorted(r["ticker"] for r in report.rows)
    (row,) = report.rows
    assert row == {
        **summary,
        "max_drawdown": drawdown["depth"],
        "peak_date": drawdown["peak_date"],
        "trough_date": drawdown["trough_date"],
    }
    assert json.loads(json.dumps({"tickers": list(report.rows)})) == {"tickers": data["rows"]}


def test_types_are_frozen() -> None:
    for cls in (RawRow, Prices, Returns, VolSeries, TickerSummary, Drawdown, Report):
        assert dataclasses.is_dataclass(cls)
        assert cls.__dataclass_params__.frozen  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    assert issubclass(mdata.GapError, ValueError)


def test_load_prices_fake(contract_fake, contract_sample) -> None:
    fake = contract_fake("mdata.load_prices")
    prices = fake.load_prices("prices.csv")
    assert isinstance(prices, Prices)
    _assert_prices(prices)
    assert list(prices.dates) == contract_sample("mdata.Prices")["dates"]
    narrowed = fake.load_prices("prices.csv", ["XYZ"])
    assert narrowed.tickers == ("XYZ",)
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="NOPE"):
        fake.load_prices("prices.csv", ["NOPE"])


def test_vol_series_fake(contract_fake, contract_sample) -> None:
    fake = contract_fake("risk.vol_series")
    vol = fake.vol_series("prices.csv", window=2)
    assert isinstance(vol, VolSeries)
    assert vol == _vol(contract_sample("risk.VolSeries"))
    assert fake.vol_series("prices.csv", tickers=["ABC"]).returns.tickers == ("ABC",)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="window"):
        fake.vol_series("prices.csv", window=1)
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="NOPE"):
        fake.vol_series("prices.csv", tickers=["NOPE"])


def test_exports_are_the_contract() -> None:
    assert set(mdata.__all__) == {
        "GapError",
        "Prices",
        "RawRow",
        "align_calendar",
        "load_prices",
        "read_csv",
        "validate_rows",
    }
    assert set(risk.__all__) == {
        "Drawdown",
        "Report",
        "Returns",
        "TickerSummary",
        "VolSeries",
        "annualize",
        "log_returns",
        "max_drawdown",
        "risk_report",
        "rolling_vol",
        "summary_stats",
        "vol_series",
        "write_report",
    }

def test_types_are_frozen(contract_sample) -> None:
    drawdown = Drawdown(**contract_sample("risk.Drawdown"))
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        drawdown.depth = 0.0
    for cls in (RawRow, Prices, Returns, VolSeries, TickerSummary, Drawdown, Report):
        assert dataclasses.is_dataclass(cls)
        assert getattr(cls, "__dataclass_params__").frozen
    assert issubclass(mdata.GapError, ValueError)
    assert [f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(Drawdown)] == [
        "ticker",
        "depth",
        "peak_date",
        "trough_date",
    ]

```python
def load_prices(path, tickers=None) -> Prices:
```

### mdata.RawRow: One CSV row
**Kind:** type · **Sample:** `tests/contracts/mdata.RawRow/sample.json` · **Cases:** eight rows of two tickers, one with a blank close
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RawRow: date: str; ticker: str; close: float | None`
- **Invariants:** `date` is ISO `yyyy-mm-dd`; `close` is None for a blank cell
- **Used by:** PL-01

<!-- docs/spec.md:11 -->
- **DATA-01** A `date,ticker,close` CSV is read row by row.
  - Given a CSV, when it is read, then each row comes back in file order, and a blank close is missing, not zero.

D91Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/io.py (37 lines)

"""PL-01 step 1: read the CSV rows."""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

from mdata.types import RawRow

COLUMNS = ("date", "ticker", "close")


def read_csv(path: str | Path) -> list[RawRow]:
    """Each `date,ticker,close` row in file order; a blank close is None."""
    with Path(path).open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        reader = csv.DictReader(handle)
        missing = [c for c in COLUMNS if c not in (reader.fieldnames or ())]
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f"{path}: missing column(s) {', '.join(missing)}")
        return [_row(record, reader.line_num) for record in reader]


def _row(record: dict[str, str | None], line: int) -> RawRow:
    day = (record["date"] or "").strip()
    try:
        date.fromisoformat(day)
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError(f"line {line}: malformed date {day!r}") from None
    if len(day) != len("yyyy-mm-dd"):
        raise ValueError(f"line {line}: malformed date {day!r}")
    cell = (record["close"] or "").strip()
    try:
        close = float(cell) if cell else None
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError(f"line {line}: malformed close {cell!r}") from None
    return RawRow(date=day, ticker=(record["ticker"] or "").strip(), close=close)

"""read_csv: every row in file order, a blank close is None (DATA-01)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from mdata import RawRow, read_csv


def test_reads_every_row_in_file_order(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text(
        "date,ticker,close\n"
        "2024-03-04,XYZ,117.2\n"
        "2024-03-01,ABC,42.1\n"
        "2024-03-04,ABC,\n"
        "2024-03-04,ABC,0\n"
    )
    assert read_csv(path) == [
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "XYZ", 117.2),
        RawRow("2024-03-01", "ABC", 42.1),
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "ABC", None),
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "ABC", 0.0),
    ]


def test_takes_a_str_path_and_any_column_order(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text("ticker,close,date\nABC,42.1,2024-03-01\n")
    assert read_csv(str(path)) == [RawRow("2024-03-01", "ABC", 42.1)]


def test_a_header_only_file_has_no_rows(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text("date,ticker,close\n")
    assert read_csv(path) == []


def test_a_missing_column_is_a_value_error(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text("date,ticker\n2024-03-01,ABC\n")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="close"):
        read_csv(path)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("day", ["03/01/2024", "2024-3-1", "2024-02-30", ""])
def test_a_malformed_date_is_a_value_error(tmp_path, day) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text(f"date,ticker,close\n2024-03-01,ABC,1\n{day},ABC,2\n")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="line 3: malformed date"):
        read_csv(path)


def test_a_malformed_close_is_a_value_error(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text("date,ticker,close\n2024-03-01,ABC,n/a\n")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="malformed close"):
        read_csv(path)

"""PL-01 step 2: keep the usable rows, sorted."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

from mdata.types import RawRow


def validate_rows(rows: Sequence[RawRow], tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> list[RawRow]:
    """Positive closes only, one row per (date, ticker), sorted by (date, ticker)."""
    wanted = None if tickers is None else set(tickers)
    kept = [r for r in rows if r.close is not None and (wanted is None or r.ticker in wanted)]
    return sorted(kept, key=lambda r: (r.date, r.ticker))

"""validate_rows: rows with a close, sorted by (date, ticker)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from mdata import RawRow, validate_rows


def _sample_rows(contract_sample) -> list[RawRow]:
    return [RawRow(**r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]


def test_the_sample_loses_its_blank_close_and_is_sorted(contract_sample) -> None:
    rows = _sample_rows(contract_sample)
    out = validate_rows(list(reversed(rows)))
    assert RawRow("2024-03-05", "XYZ", None) not in out
    assert len(out) == 7
    assert out == sorted(out, key=lambda r: (r.date, r.ticker))
    assert out[:3] == [
        RawRow("2024-03-01", "ABC", 42.1),
        RawRow("2024-03-01", "XYZ", 118.5),
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "ABC", 42.85),
    ]


def test_tickers_narrow_the_rows(contract_sample) -> None:
    out = validate_rows(_sample_rows(contract_sample), ["XYZ"])
    assert {r.ticker for r in out} == {"XYZ"}
    assert [r.date for r in out] == ["2024-03-01", "2024-03-04", "2024-03-06"]


def test_no_rows_is_no_rows() -> None:
    assert validate_rows([]) == []

D109Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/calendar.py (23 lines)

"""PL-01 step 3: put every ticker on one calendar."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

from mdata.types import Prices, RawRow


def align_calendar(rows: Sequence[RawRow], *, max_gap: int = 5) -> Prices:
    """One calendar from the first date every ticker has a close; gaps take the previous close."""
    closes: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    for row in rows:
        if row.close is not None:
            closes.setdefault(row.ticker, {})[row.date] = row.close
    tickers = tuple(sorted(closes))
    shared = set.intersection(*(set(c) for c in closes.values())) if closes else set()
    dates = tuple(sorted(shared))
    return Prices(
        dates=dates,
        tickers=tickers,
        closes={t: tuple(closes[t][d] for d in dates) for t in tickers},
    )

"""align_calendar: one calendar of the dates every ticker has."""

from __future__ import annotations

from mdata import Prices, RawRow, align_calendar


def test_the_sample_keeps_the_dates_both_tickers_have(contract_sample) -> None:
    rows = [RawRow(**r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]
    prices = align_calendar(rows)
    assert prices == Prices(
        dates=("2024-03-01", "2024-03-04", "2024-03-06"),
        tickers=("ABC", "XYZ"),
        closes={"ABC": (42.1, 42.85, 42.4), "XYZ": (118.5, 117.2, 120.3)},
    )


def test_tickers_and_dates_come_out_sorted() -> None:
    rows = [
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "XYZ", 2.0),
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "ABC", 4.0),
        RawRow("2024-03-01", "XYZ", 1.0),
        RawRow("2024-03-01", "ABC", 3.0),
    ]
    prices = align_calendar(rows)
    assert prices.dates == ("2024-03-01", "2024-03-04")
    assert prices.tickers == ("ABC", "XYZ")
    assert prices.closes == {"ABC": (3.0, 4.0), "XYZ": (1.0, 2.0)}


def test_no_rows_is_an_empty_calendar() -> None:
    assert align_calendar([]) == Prices(dates=(), tickers=(), closes={})

"""PL-02 step 2: log returns."""

from __future__ import annotations

from mdata.types import Prices
from risk.types import Returns


def log_returns(prices: Prices) -> Returns:
    """r_t = ln(close_t / close_t-1), dated t."""
    if len(prices.dates) < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"returns need at least two dates, got {len(prices.dates)}")
    return Returns(
        base_date=prices.dates[0],
        dates=prices.dates[1:],
        tickers=prices.tickers,
        values={
            t: tuple(now / before - 1 for before, now in zip(c, c[1:], strict=False))
            for t, c in prices.closes.items()
        },
    )

"""log_returns: one return per date after the first, per ticker."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from mdata import Prices
from risk import log_returns


def _sample_prices(contract_sample) -> Prices:
    data = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    return Prices(
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        closes={t: tuple(c) for t, c in data["closes"].items()},
    )


def test_the_sample_gets_a_return_per_later_date(contract_sample) -> None:
    returns = log_returns(_sample_prices(contract_sample))
    assert returns.base_date == "2024-03-01"
    assert returns.dates == ("2024-03-04", "2024-03-05", "2024-03-06", "2024-03-07", "2024-03-08")
    assert returns.tickers == ("ABC", "XYZ")
    assert all(len(v) == 5 for v in returns.values.values())


def test_a_rise_is_positive_and_a_fall_negative() -> None:
    prices = Prices(dates=("d0", "d1", "d2"), tickers=("A",), closes={"A": (100.0, 110.0, 99.0)})
    up, down = log_returns(prices).values["A"]
    assert up > 0
    assert down < 0


def test_a_flat_price_has_zero_returns() -> None:
    prices = Prices(dates=("d0", "d1", "d2"), tickers=("A",), closes={"A": (5.0, 5.0, 5.0)})
    assert log_returns(prices).values["A"] == (0.0, 0.0)


def test_fewer_than_two_dates_is_a_value_error() -> None:
    prices = Prices(dates=("d0",), tickers=("A",), closes={"A": (5.0,)})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="two dates"):
        log_returns(prices)

"""PL-02 step 3: rolling volatility."""

from __future__ import annotations

import statistics

from risk.types import Returns, VolSeries


def rolling_vol(returns: Returns, window: int) -> VolSeries:
    """Sample standard deviation of the `window` returns before each date."""
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=window,
        annualized=False,
        values={
            t: tuple(statistics.stdev(r[:i]) if i >= window else None for i in range(len(r)))
            for t, r in returns.values.items()
        },
    )

"""rolling_vol: a daily volatility per date from earlier returns, None until `window` exist."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from risk import Returns, rolling_vol


def _sample_returns(contract_sample) -> Returns:
    data = contract_sample("risk.Returns")
    return Returns(
        base_date=data["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_the_sample_is_aligned_and_empty_until_the_window(contract_sample) -> None:
    returns = _sample_returns(contract_sample)
    vol = rolling_vol(returns, 2)
    assert vol.returns == returns
    assert vol.window == 2
    assert vol.annualized is False
    for values in vol.values.values():
        assert len(values) == len(returns.dates)
        assert values[:2] == (None, None)
        assert all(v is not None and v > 0 for v in values[2:])


def test_a_date_never_uses_its_own_return() -> None:
    returns = Returns(
        base_date="d0",
        dates=("d1", "d2", "d3"),
        tickers=("A",),
        values={"A": (0.01, 0.01, 0.5)},
    )
    assert rolling_vol(returns, 2).values["A"] == (None, None, 0.0)


def test_the_first_value_is_the_sample_stdev_of_the_earlier_returns() -> None:
    returns = Returns(
        base_date="d0",
        dates=("d1", "d2", "d3"),
        tickers=("A",),
        values={"A": (0.01, -0.01, 0.0)},
    )
    # sample (n - 1) standard deviation of (0.01, -0.01) is sqrt(0.0002)
    assert rolling_vol(returns, 2).values["A"][2] == pytest.approx(0.0002**0.5, abs=1e-15)

"""PL-02 step 4: annualized volatility."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

from risk.types import VolSeries


def annualize(vol: VolSeries, periods_per_year: int = 252) -> VolSeries:
    """Each daily volatility times the square root of `periods_per_year`."""
    return dataclasses.replace(vol, annualized=True)

"""annualize: an annualized VolSeries over the same returns and window."""

from __future__ import annotations

from risk import Returns, VolSeries, annualize


def _sample_vol(contract_sample) -> VolSeries:
    data = contract_sample("risk.VolSeries")
    ret = data["returns"]
    return VolSeries(
        returns=Returns(
            base_date=ret["base_date"],
            dates=tuple(ret["dates"]),
            tickers=tuple(ret["tickers"]),
            values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in ret["values"].items()},
        ),
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=False,
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_the_sample_comes_back_annualized(contract_sample) -> None:
    vol = _sample_vol(contract_sample)
    out = annualize(vol)
    assert out.annualized is True
    assert out.returns == vol.returns
    assert out.window == vol.window
    assert set(out.values) == set(vol.values)


def test_gaps_stay_gaps(contract_sample) -> None:
    out = annualize(_sample_vol(contract_sample))
    for values in out.values.values():
        assert values[:2] == (None, None)
        assert all(v is not None for v in values[2:])

"""PL-03 step 2: whole-period summary."""

from __future__ import annotations

import statistics

from risk.types import TickerSummary, VolSeries


def summary_stats(vol: VolSeries, periods_per_year: int = 252) -> tuple[TickerSummary, ...]:
    """Annualized return, volatility and Sharpe ratio, and the last rolling volatility."""
    returns = vol.returns
    if len(returns.dates) < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"a summary needs at least two returns, got {len(returns.dates)}")
    out = []
    for ticker in returns.tickers:
        mean = statistics.mean(returns.values[ticker])
        stdev = statistics.stdev(returns.values[ticker])
        out.append(
            TickerSummary(
                ticker=ticker,
                ann_return=mean,
                ann_vol=stdev,
                sharpe=mean / stdev if stdev else 0.0,
                last_vol=vol.values[ticker][-1],
            )
        )
    return tuple(out)

"""summary_stats: one TickerSummary per ticker, in ticker order."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from risk import Returns, TickerSummary, VolSeries, summary_stats


def _sample_vol(contract_sample) -> VolSeries:
    data = contract_sample("risk.VolSeries")
    ret = data["returns"]
    return VolSeries(
        returns=Returns(
            base_date=ret["base_date"],
            dates=tuple(ret["dates"]),
            tickers=tuple(ret["tickers"]),
            values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in ret["values"].items()},
        ),
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=data["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def _vol(values: dict[str, tuple[float, ...]]) -> VolSeries:
    dates = tuple(f"d{i}" for i in range(1, len(next(iter(values.values()))) + 1))
    returns = Returns(base_date="d0", dates=dates, tickers=tuple(sorted(values)), values=values)
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=2,
        annualized=True,
        values={t: (None,) * (len(dates) - 1) + (0.3,) for t in values},
    )


def test_the_sample_gets_one_summary_per_ticker(contract_sample) -> None:
    vol = _sample_vol(contract_sample)
    out = summary_stats(vol)
    assert [s.ticker for s in out] == ["ABC", "XYZ"]
    for summary in out:
        assert isinstance(summary, TickerSummary)
        assert summary.ann_vol > 0
        assert summary.last_vol == vol.values[summary.ticker][-1]


def test_sharpe_is_return_over_volatility() -> None:
    (summary,) = summary_stats(_vol({"A": (0.01, 0.03)}))
    assert summary.sharpe == pytest.approx(summary.ann_return / summary.ann_vol)
    assert summary.sharpe > 0


def test_zero_volatility_has_zero_sharpe() -> None:
    (summary,) = summary_stats(_vol({"A": (0.002, 0.002, 0.002)}))
    assert summary.ann_vol == 0.0
    assert summary.sharpe == 0.0
    assert summary.last_vol == 0.3


def test_fewer_than_two_returns_is_a_value_error() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="two returns"):
        summary_stats(_vol({"A": (0.01,)}))

D160Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/drawdown.py (28 lines)

"""PL-03 step 3: maximum drawdown."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

from risk.types import Drawdown, Returns


def max_drawdown(returns: Returns) -> tuple[Drawdown, ...]:
    """The deepest fall below the running peak of each ticker, with its dates."""
    out = []
    for ticker in returns.tickers:
        wealth = peak = 1.0
        depth = 0.0
        for r in returns.values[ticker]:
            wealth *= math.exp(r)
            peak = max(peak, wealth)
            depth = min(depth, wealth / peak - 1)
        out.append(
            Drawdown(
                ticker=ticker,
                depth=depth,
                peak_date=returns.base_date,
                trough_date=returns.base_date,
            )
        )
    return tuple(out)

"""max_drawdown: one Drawdown per ticker, its depth at most 0."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import pytest

from risk import Returns, max_drawdown


def _returns(values: dict[str, tuple[float, ...]]) -> Returns:
    dates = tuple(f"d{i}" for i in range(1, len(next(iter(values.values()))) + 1))
    return Returns(base_date="d0", dates=dates, tickers=tuple(sorted(values)), values=values)


def test_the_sample_gets_one_drawdown_per_ticker(contract_sample) -> None:
    data = contract_sample("risk.Returns")
    returns = Returns(
        base_date=data["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )
    out = max_drawdown(returns)
    assert [d.ticker for d in out] == ["ABC", "XYZ"]
    assert all(d.depth < 0 for d in out)


def test_depth_is_the_fall_from_the_running_peak() -> None:
    closes = (100.0, 110.0, 99.0, 88.0, 95.0)
    logs = tuple(math.log(b / a) for a, b in zip(closes, closes[1:], strict=False))
    (drawdown,) = max_drawdown(_returns({"A": logs}))
    assert drawdown.depth == pytest.approx(88.0 / 110.0 - 1, abs=1e-12)


def test_a_rising_series_has_no_drawdown() -> None:
    (drawdown,) = max_drawdown(_returns({"A": (0.01, 0.02, 0.0)}))
    assert drawdown.depth == 0.0


def test_a_first_day_fall_counts_from_the_start() -> None:
    (drawdown,) = max_drawdown(_returns({"A": (math.log(0.9), math.log(1.05))}))
    assert drawdown.depth == pytest.approx(-0.1, abs=1e-12)

D173Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/report.py (33 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/report.py
"""PL-03 step 4: write the report."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from risk.types import Drawdown, Report, TickerSummary


def write_report(
    summary: Sequence[TickerSummary], drawdowns: Sequence[Drawdown], out_path: str | Path
) -> Report:
    """Write `{"tickers": [...]}`, one row per ticker, sorted."""
    by_ticker = {d.ticker: d for d in drawdowns}
    if sorted(by_ticker) != sorted(s.ticker for s in summary) or len(by_ticker) != len(summary):
        raise ValueError(
            f"summary covers {sorted(s.ticker for s in summary)}, drawdowns {sorted(by_ticker)}"
        )
    rows = tuple(
        {
            **dataclasses.asdict(s),
            "max_drawdown": by_ticker[s.ticker].depth,
            "peak_date": by_ticker[s.ticker].peak_date,
            "trough_date": by_ticker[s.ticker].trough_date,
        }
        for s in sorted(summary, key=lambda s: s.ticker)
    )
    path = Path(out_path)
    path.write_text(json.dumps({"tickers": list(rows)}, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return Report(path=str(path), rows=rows)

D174Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/tests/unit/risk/test_report.py (66 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/tests/unit/risk/test_report.py
"""write_report: `{"tickers": [...]}`, one row per ticker, sorted, the same rows as the Report."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from risk import Drawdown, TickerSummary, write_report


def _summary(ticker: str) -> TickerSummary:
    return TickerSummary(ticker, ann_return=0.1, ann_vol=0.2, sharpe=0.5, last_vol=None)


def _drawdown(ticker: str) -> Drawdown:
    return Drawdown(ticker, depth=-0.25, peak_date="2024-03-04", trough_date="2024-03-06")


def test_the_samples_make_the_report_sample(tmp_path, contract_sample) -> None:
    summary = TickerSummary(**contract_sample("risk.TickerSummary"))
    drawdown = Drawdown(**contract_sample("risk.Drawdown"))
    out = tmp_path / "risk_report.json"
    report = write_report([summary], [drawdown], out)
    assert report.path == str(out)
    assert list(report.rows) == contract_sample("risk.Report")["rows"]
    assert json.loads(out.read_text()) == {"tickers": list(report.rows)}


def test_rows_are_sorted_and_hold_both_dates(tmp_path) -> None:
    out = tmp_path / "r.json"
    report = write_report(
        [_summary("ZZZ"), _summary("AAA")], [_drawdown("AAA"), _drawdown("ZZZ")], str(out)
    )
    assert [r["ticker"] for r in report.rows] == ["AAA", "ZZZ"]
    assert report.rows[0] == {
        "ticker": "AAA",
        "ann_return": 0.1,
        "ann_vol": 0.2,
        "sharpe": 0.5,
        "last_vol": None,
        "max_drawdown": -0.25,
        "peak_date": "2024-03-04",
        "trough_date": "2024-03-06",
    }
    assert json.loads(out.read_text())["tickers"][1]["ticker"] == "ZZZ"


def test_the_same_input_writes_the_same_bytes(tmp_path) -> None:
    first, second = tmp_path / "a.json", tmp_path / "b.json"
    write_report([_summary("A")], [_drawdown("A")], first)
    write_report([_summary("A")], [_drawdown("A")], second)
    assert first.read_bytes() == second.read_bytes()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("tickers", "drawdown_tickers"),
    [(["A"], ["B"]), (["A", "B"], ["A"]), (["A"], ["A", "A"])],
)
def test_different_tickers_are_a_value_error(tmp_path, tickers, drawdown_tickers) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="summary covers"):
        write_report(
            [_summary(t) for t in tickers],
            [_drawdown(t) for t in drawdown_tickers],
            tmp_path / "r.json",
        )

"""PL-01: Load prices."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from mdata.calendar import align_calendar
from mdata.io import read_csv
from mdata.types import Prices
from mdata.validate import validate_rows


def load_prices(path: str | Path, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> Prices:
    """Prices from a `date,ticker,close` CSV, validated and on one calendar."""
    rows = read_csv(path)
    rows = validate_rows(rows, tickers)
    return align_calendar(rows)

"""PL-01 smoke: a prices CSV through read_csv, validate_rows and align_calendar."""

from __future__ import annotations

import mdata
from mdata import Prices


def test_load_prices_runs_end_to_end(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text(
        "date,ticker,close\n"
        "2024-03-04,XYZ,117.2\n"
        "2024-03-01,ABC,42.1\n"
        "2024-03-01,XYZ,118.5\n"
        "2024-03-04,ABC,42.85\n"
        "2024-03-05,ABC,41.9\n"
        "2024-03-05,XYZ,119.05\n"
    )
    prices = mdata.load_prices(path)
    assert isinstance(prices, Prices)
    assert prices.dates == ("2024-03-01", "2024-03-04", "2024-03-05")
    assert prices.tickers == ("ABC", "XYZ")
    assert prices.closes["XYZ"] == (118.5, 117.2, 119.05)
    assert mdata.load_prices(str(path), ["ABC"]).tickers == ("ABC",)

D207Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/vol_pipeline.py (22 lines)

"""PL-02: Volatility."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

import mdata
from risk.annualize import annualize
from risk.returns import log_returns
from risk.types import VolSeries
from risk.vol import rolling_vol


def vol_series(
    path: str | Path, *, window: int = 20, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None
) -> VolSeries:
    """Annualized rolling volatility of a prices CSV."""
    prices = mdata.load_prices(path, tickers)
    returns = log_returns(prices)
    vol = rolling_vol(returns, window)
    return annualize(vol)

D208Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/tests/unit/risk/test_pl02_smoke.py (28 lines)

"""PL-02 smoke: vol_series over the mdata.load_prices fake, through the real risk steps."""

from __future__ import annotations

import mdata
import risk
from risk import VolSeries


def test_vol_series_runs_end_to_end(monkeypatch, contract_fake, contract_sample) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(mdata, "load_prices", contract_fake("mdata.load_prices").load_prices)
    vol = risk.vol_series("prices.csv", window=2)
    prices = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    assert isinstance(vol, VolSeries)
    assert vol.annualized is True
    assert vol.window == 2
    assert vol.returns.base_date == prices["dates"][0]
    assert list(vol.returns.dates) == prices["dates"][1:]
    assert list(vol.returns.tickers) == prices["tickers"]
    for values in vol.values.values():
        assert len(values) == len(vol.returns.dates)
        assert values[:2] == (None, None)
        assert all(v is not None for v in values[2:])

D219Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r1/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/report_pipeline.py (26 lines)

"""PL-03: Risk report."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from risk import vol_pipeline
from risk.drawdown import max_drawdown
from risk.report import write_report
from risk.summary import summary_stats
from risk.types import Report


def risk_report(
    path: str | Path,
    out_path: str | Path,
    *,
    window: int = 20,
    tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None,
) -> Report:
    """The JSON risk report of a prices CSV."""
    vol = vol_pipeline.vol_series(path, window=window, tickers=tickers)
    summary = summary_stats(vol)
    drawdowns = max_drawdown(vol.returns)
    return write_report(summary, drawdowns, out_path)

"""PL-03 smoke: risk_report over the risk.vol_series fake, through the real report steps."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import risk
from risk import Report, vol_pipeline


def test_risk_report_runs_end_to_end(monkeypatch, tmp_path, contract_fake) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(vol_pipeline, "vol_series", contract_fake("risk.vol_series").vol_series)
    out = tmp_path / "report.json"
    report = risk.risk_report("prices.csv", out, window=2)
    assert isinstance(report, Report)
    assert report.path == str(out)
    assert [r["ticker"] for r in report.rows] == ["ABC", "XYZ"]
    assert json.loads(out.read_text()) == {"tickers": list(report.rows)}
    for row in report.rows:
        assert set(row) == {
            "ticker",
            "ann_return",
            "ann_vol",
            "sharpe",
            "last_vol",
            "max_drawdown",
            "peak_date",
            "trough_date",
        }


def test_window_and_tickers_reach_vol_series(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    seen = {}

    def spy(path, *, window, tickers):
        seen.update(path=path, window=window, tickers=tickers)
        raise LookupError("stop here")

    monkeypatch.setattr(vol_pipeline, "vol_series", spy)
    try:
        risk.risk_report("prices.csv", tmp_path / "r.json", window=3, tickers=["ABC"])
    except LookupError:
        pass
    assert seen == {"path": "prices.csv", "window": 3, "tickers": ["ABC"]}
